## Leads

_Suspicions another session met and handed over, one line each; the
upstream session works them (MAINTENANCE upstream session step 3) and
deletes each once it is a report, a register entry or dismissed._


- 2026-10-08 (PR review of pkp/pkp-lib#13479, slot s3): OJS's `plugins/generic/jatsTemplate/tests/functional/ArticleBackTest` fails its "Validates against jats 12" case on the VM at the ojs tip `ae8dbb47d7` with lib/pkp `2ac457888e`, before the PR ("JATS 1.2 DTD Validation failed: Line 1: No declaration for attribute xlink:href of element ext-link", from a data citation's `<ext-link>`; the other three cases pass; `.reports/pr13479/unit/base-jats.log`). Not looked into: whether pkp's own `ojs-main` jobs show it, and whether the back matter's `ext-link` lacks the xlink namespace in the XML or only in the test's validation.
